Copa del Bicenteneario de la Indenpendencia

NB: between champions of Campeonato 2014 and Campeonato 2016.
Cup played once to celebrate the 200th anniversary of Argentina’s independence.
Champion 2014: Racing Club Asociación Civil.
Champion 2016: Club Atlético Lanús.
The match had to be played on neutral field, but both clubs agreed to choose by lot between their fields.

Notes:
. Alianza de los Mandiyú: Alliance of ‘Club Deportivo Mandiyú’ and ‘Club Social y Deportivo Textil Mandiyú’.
The place belonged to the last, the alliance pact was signed a few days before the beginning of the tournament, then, AFA used the name Textil Mandiyú in the fixture.
. Club Camioneros Argentinos del Norte: Alliance of ‘Sindicato de Choferes de Camiones, Obreros y Empleados del Transporte Automotor de Cargas’ and
‘Club Atlético Argentinos del Norte’. As the traditional club is the last, AFA used its name in the fixture.
. Puerto Gral. San Martín: Alliance of ‘Polideportivo Puerto General San Martín’ and ‘Municipalidad de Puerto Gral. San Martín’.
Also known as PSM Fútbol Social y Deportivo.
. General Rojo Unión Deportiva: Alliance of ‘Club Atlético Defensores de Mataderos’, ‘Club Social y Deportivo Boulevard’, ‘Club Social Democrático’,
‘Delegación Municipal General Rojo del Partido de San Nicolás de los Arroyos’, ‘Coop. Eléctrica Ltda. de Gral. Rojo’, ‘Coop. Agrícola de Gral. Rojo Ltda.’,
‘Cooperativa de Agua Potable y otros Servicios Públicos de General Rojo Ltda.’, and,
‘Unión Argentina de Trabajadores Rurales y Estibadores - UATRE - Seccional General Rojo’.

Copa Provincia de Santa Fe

AFA 5120 – Federación Santafesina de Fútbol (Santa Fe Football Federation) founded “Copa Provincia de Santa Fe”.
           Consejo Federal (Federal Council) approved it.
           AFA took knowledge it.
To be played by Santa Fe teams which played national, metropolitan and federal tournaments, and champions of Santafesinian regional leagues.
